(async()=>{try{
const available=await figma.listAvailableFontsAsync();
function pick(preferred){for(const name of preferred){const f=available.find(v=>v.fontName.family===name&&/regular|normal|book/i.test(v.fontName.style));if(f)return f.fontName}return {family:'Inter',style:'Regular'}}
const serif=pick(['SimSun','Noto Serif SC','Noto Serif CJK SC','Source Han Serif SC','Songti SC']);
const sans=pick(['Microsoft YaHei','Noto Sans SC','Noto Sans CJK SC','PingFang SC','Inter']);
await figma.loadFontAsync(serif);await figma.loadFontAsync(sans);let x=0;const frames=[];
for(const b of boards){const frame=figma.createNodeFromSvg(b.geometry);frame.name='雨季 / '+b.name;frame.x=x;frame.y=0;frame.clipsContent=false;
for(const t of b.texts){const n=figma.createText();n.fontName=t.serif?serif:sans;n.fontSize=t.size;n.characters=t.text;n.textAutoResize='WIDTH_AND_HEIGHT';n.letterSpacing={unit:'PIXELS',value:t.spacing};const c=t.color.replace('#','');n.fills=[{type:'SOLID',color:{r:parseInt(c.slice(0,2),16)/255,g:parseInt(c.slice(2,4),16)/255,b:parseInt(c.slice(4,6),16)/255}}];frame.appendChild(n);n.x=t.x;n.y=t.y-t.size*.88;n.name=t.text}
frames.push(frame);x+=frame.width+100}
figma.currentPage.selection=frames;figma.viewport.scrollAndZoomIntoView(frames);figma.closePlugin('已创建 4 张画板：文字为原生 Text，图形为可编辑矢量。');
}catch(e){figma.closePlugin('导入未完成：'+String(e))}})();
